# Skills · C · part 9

887 entries, channel-performance-review to check-agent-compatibility. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **channel-performance-review**: Use when the merchant asks which channels, sources, media, campaigns, or devices are driving revenue, customers, or on-hold pipeline. - **cheat-score-blind**: INTERNAL sub-agent for blind 7-dim rubric scoring. **NOT a user-facing skill — do NOT invoke from main conversation.** Called via Task tool by cheat-score / cheat-predict / cheat-bump to get a context-isolated score on a script.
